\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right];

(177)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,2}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}\times\left\|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]-\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)\right\|_{\max}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t}\Big];

(178)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,3}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]-\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)\right\|_{\max}^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right].

(179)

Notice that we have the following decomposition:

\displaystyle Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)-Q^{*}_{i}(s,\mathbf{a})

(180)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a}))(Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}_{z^{*}}Q^{*}_{i}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a}))(Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})+\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}_{z^{*}}Q^{*}_{i}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a}))(Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})(\mathcal{T}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)

where we have used the fact that
\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}Q_{i,t}=\mathcal{T}Q_{i,t},\mathcal{T}_{z^{*}}Q_{i}^{*}=\mathcal{T}Q_{i}^{*}
in the last equality. Therefore, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}

(181)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i,s,\mathbf{a}}\{Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)-Q^{*}_{i}(s,\mathbf{a})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i,s,\mathbf{a}}\{(1-\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a}))(Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})(\mathcal{T}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}Q_{i}^{*}(s,\mathbf{a}))

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\gamma_{0}\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}

where
d_{t+1,\min{}}=\min_{s,\mathbf{a}}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})
and
d_{t+1,\max{}}=\max_{s,\mathbf{a}}d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})
.
We can now rewrite the non-stochastic drift term
\mathcal{L}_{1,1}
as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,1}

(182)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left((1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{1})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{1}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}

where we have used (
181
) in the first inequality and
Appendix

I
in the second inequality. Recall
Section

G.2
and
Section

G.2
, we have that (notice that we are still taking maximum over all
s\in\mathcal{S}
for
z
, such that
\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}=\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|z_{t}(\cdot|s)-z_{t}^{*}(\cdot|s)\|_{2}
):

\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}\leq\gamma^{2}C^{2}_{\mathcal{T}}\|z_{t+1}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}\leq\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}.

(183)

Combining (
182
) and (
183
) we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,1}\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{1})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

(184)

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{1}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}.

For term
\mathcal{L}_{1,2}
, recall that
\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\Pr((s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t})
, by
Section

4.3
, we know that
\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})
converges to
d_{t+1}
with a rate of (here we focus on the on-policy case, the same proof goes through for the off-policy case as well):

\displaystyle|\Pr((s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t})-d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})|=

\displaystyle|P_{\pi_{t+1}}^{k}(s_{0},s|\mathcal{F}_{t})\pi_{t+1}(\mathbf{a}|s)-d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})|

(185)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|P_{\pi_{t+1}}^{k}(s_{0},s|\mathcal{F}_{t})-\mu_{\pi_{t+1}}(\cdot)\|_{\mathrm{TV}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\rho^{k}

and therefore:

\displaystyle|\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})-d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})|

(186)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\Pr((s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t})-d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}|\Pr((s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t})-d_{t+1}(s,\mathbf{a})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\rho^{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{K}\frac{1-\rho^{K}}{1-\rho}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}.

Recall (
163
), we have the following upper bound on
\mathcal{L}_{1,2}
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,2}

(187)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}\times\left\|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]-\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)\right\|_{\max}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}\times\left\|(\bar{P}_{K}(\cdot,\cdot|\mathcal{F}_{t})-\mathbf{d}_{t+1})\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)\right\|_{\max}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{d}_{t+1}\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\right\|_{\max}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t}\Big]\times\left\|(\bar{P}_{K}(\cdot,\cdot|\mathcal{F}_{t})-\mathbf{d}_{t+1})\odot\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\right)\right\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\left((1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)

\displaystyle\times\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{2}\left((1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}+\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{2}(1+c_{3})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}+\frac{c_{2}}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)^{2}

where the (i) holds by (
181
), (ii) holds because
ab=\sqrt{c_{2}}a\cdot\frac{b}{\sqrt{c_{2}}}<\frac{c_{2}}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}b^{2}
and (iii) uses
Appendix

I
. We further apply (
183
) and
Section

H.1
to the last line and and obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,2}

(188)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{2}(1+c_{3})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{2}}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left((1+c_{4})\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{4}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}(1+c_{3})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{4}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}+\frac{1+c_{4}}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}(1+c_{3})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}+\frac{1}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{4}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{c_{2}}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}+\frac{1+c_{4}}{2c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

Similarly, for term
\mathcal{L}_{1,3}
, combining (
163
) and (
186
) we obtain:

\mathcal{L}_{1,3}\leq\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\right)^{2},

(189)

which can be further transformed using (
183
) and
Section

H.1
to:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,3}\leq\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left((1+c_{5})\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathcal{T}_{z_{t}^{*}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|^{2}_{\max}+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{5}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}\right)

(190)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left((1+c_{5})\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{5}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}\right).

Having each individual term bounded in (
184
), (
188
) and (
190
) respectively, we can now sum them up to get an overall bound for
\mathcal{L}_{1}
using (
176
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}

(191)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,1}+2\alpha_{t}\mathcal{L}_{1,2}+\alpha_{t}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{1,3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{1})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{1}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}\left(c_{2}(1+c_{3})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{4}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}\left(c_{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}+\frac{(1+c_{4})}{c_{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1+c_{5})\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{5}}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg((1+c_{1}+c_{2}\alpha_{t}(1+c_{3}))(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{c_{2}}\alpha_{t}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{4}}\right)+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{5}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\Bigg)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{1}}+c_{2}\alpha_{t}\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{3}}\right)\right)\alpha^{2}_{t}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}

\displaystyle+(\frac{1}{c_{2}}\alpha_{t}(1+c_{4})+\alpha_{t}^{2}(1+c_{5}))\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\Bigg)\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

For simplicity, we take
c_{1}=c\alpha_{t}
,
c_{2}=\frac{c}{2}
where
c=\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}
and
c_{3}=c_{4}=c_{5}=1
, when
K\geq\frac{2\sqrt{2}C}{(1-\rho)(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}\leq

(192)

\displaystyle(1+(c+2c_{2})\alpha_{t})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}+2(\frac{1}{c_{2}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{c}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}+(1+2c_{2}\alpha_{t})\alpha_{t}^{2}d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{1}{c_{2}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{4}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\alpha_{t}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})\alpha_{t}^{2}d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{4}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-(1-\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}d_{t+1,\min{}})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{4}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\alpha_{t}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}d^{2}_{t+1,\max{}}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})\alpha_{t}^{2}d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2(\frac{4}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\alpha_{t}+\alpha_{t}^{2})\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\left((1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}-\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}+\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\left((1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

Bounding the additional error terms
\mathcal{L}_{2},\mathcal{L}_{3}
and
\mathcal{L}_{4}
.

We now focus on the additional bias terms
\mathcal{L}_{2},\mathcal{L}_{3}
and
\mathcal{L}_{4}
. Since each of the terms is in the order of
\alpha_{t}^{2}
, we only need to bound the scales of each term in order to obtain the convergence result. For
\mathcal{L}_{2}
, notice that
\mathcal{L}_{2}
can be obtained by directly taking max-norm on
\mathcal{L}_{2}^{\prime}
, we analyze
\mathcal{L}_{2}^{\prime}
instead. Recall the definition of
\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})
and (
163
), we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}^{\prime}

(193)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})-Q_{i,t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})\right)\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\delta}_{i}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)\left(\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

The term inside the expectation can be bounded using geometric ergodicity: Let
Y_{k}=\mathbf{1}[(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})]-\Pr((s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})=(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t})

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

(194)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}Y_{k}\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\textup{Var}(Y_{k})+\sum_{0\leq k_{1}\neq k_{2}\leq K-1}\textup{Cov}(Y_{k_{1}},Y_{k_{2}})\right)

Since
\mathbb{E}[Y_{k}]=0
and
Y_{k}
only takes two values, we have
\textup{Var}(Y_{k})\leq\frac{1}{4}
. For
\textup{Cov}(Y_{k_{1}},Y_{k_{2}})
we obtain from
Appendix

I
and
Appendix

I
that

\textup{Cov}(Y_{k_{1}},Y_{k_{2}})\leq 8C\rho^{|k_{1}-k_{2}|}

(195)

combining the two equations above, we have:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathbf{1}[(s,\mathbf{a})=(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})]-\bar{P}_{K}(s,\mathbf{a}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]\leq\frac{1}{4K}+\frac{16C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}

(196)

and as a result,

\mathcal{L}_{2}^{\prime}\leq\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})-Q_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{4K}+\frac{16C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right).

(197)

Now we take max-norm and obtain the bound for
\mathcal{L}_{2}
:

\mathcal{L}_{2}\leq\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{4K}+\frac{16C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right)\|\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}^{2}.

(198)

Using (
183
) and
Section

H.1
with
c=1
we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2K}+\frac{32C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right)\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

(199)

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2K}+\frac{32C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

For
\mathcal{L}_{3}
, recall the definition
M_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\xi_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})
and the property that
\mathbb{E}[M_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]=0
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[M^{2}_{i,t}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\xi_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

(200)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]+\frac{1}{K^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k_{1}\neq k_{2}}^{K-1}\xi_{i,k_{1}}(s,\mathbf{a})\xi_{i,k_{2}}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]+\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k_{1}\neq k_{2}}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{i,k_{1}}(s,\mathbf{a})\xi_{i,k_{2}}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]+\frac{2}{K^{2}}\sum_{k_{1}<k_{2}}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i,k_{1}}(s,\mathbf{a})\xi_{i,k_{2}}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t,k_{1}}]\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]+\frac{2}{K^{2}}\sum_{k_{1}<k_{2}}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i,k_{2}}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t,k_{1}}]\xi_{i,k_{1}}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]+\frac{2}{K^{2}}\sum_{k_{1}<k_{2}}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i,k_{2}}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t,k_{2}}]|\mathcal{F}_{t,k_{1}}]\xi_{i,k_{1}}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

and as a result,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\xi_{i,k}(s,\mathbf{a})\right)^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i,k}^{2}(s,\mathbf{a})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K}\max_{k}\|\xi_{i,k}\|_{\max}^{2}

(201)

and since this bound hold for all
(s,\mathbf{a})
pairs, we have:

\mathcal{L}_{3}\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K}\max_{k}\|\xi_{i,k}\|_{\max}^{2}\leq\frac{4\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{Q})^{2}.

(202)

For
\mathcal{L}_{4}
, by using the fact that
ab\leq\frac{1}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2}b^{2}
, we obtain:

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{L}_{4}=

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})-\mathbf{Q}_{t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})\right)\otimes e_{(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})}-\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\right\|_{\max}\times\left\|\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{k}\right\|_{\max}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

(203)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{z_{t+1}}\mathbf{Q}_{t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})-\mathbf{Q}_{t}(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})\right)\otimes e_{(s_{k},\mathbf{a}_{k})}-\alpha_{t}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\right\|^{2}_{\max}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{t}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{k}\right\|^{2}_{\max}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}+\mathcal{L}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2K}+\frac{32C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right)\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2K}+\frac{32C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}\right)(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}+\frac{4\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{Q})^{2}

Obtaining the Lyapunov Drift Inequality for
Q
.

Having obtained the upper bounds for
\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2},\mathcal{L}_{3}
and
\mathcal{L}_{4}
, we finally combine the error terms to obtain the Lyapunov drift inequality for
Q
functions. Recall (
168
) and substituting in (
192
), (
199
), (
202
), and (
203
), we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t+1}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]

(204)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}+\mathcal{L}_{2}+\mathcal{L}_{3}+2\mathcal{L}_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\left((1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}-\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}^{2}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2d_{t+1,\max{}}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\alpha_{t}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\left((1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}+\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\alpha_{t}^{2}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2})\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{8\alpha_{t}^{2}}{K}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{Q})^{2}

H.2
Lyapunov Drift Inequality for Policies

We now shift our focus on the Lyapunov drift inequality for policies. Notice that given the
Q
function
\mathbf{Q}_{t}
at time step
t
, the policy update is exactly the same as the non-stochastic update rule (
17
) because:

\Lambda F(z;-\mathbf{Q}_{t}(s,\cdot))=\begin{bmatrix}\lambda_{1}(Q_{1}(s,\cdot)p_{1}(\cdot|s)+\epsilon_{1}\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1};s))\\
\lambda_{2}(Q_{2}(s,\cdot)p_{2}(\cdot|s)+\epsilon_{2}\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2};s))\\
\lambda_{1}(-Q_{1}^{T}(s,\cdot)\pi_{1}(\cdot|s)+\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2};s))\\
\lambda_{2}(-Q_{2}^{T}(s,\cdot)\pi_{2}(\cdot|s)+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1};s))\end{bmatrix}

(205)

this provides the same iteration dynamic as (
129
):

\|z_{t+1}-z_{t+1}^{*}\|_{2}\leq(1+\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}C_{\mathcal{T}})\sqrt{1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2}}\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}+(1+\gamma_{0})\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}.

(206)

However, in order to fit the squared term in the drift inequality for
Q
functions, we apply
Appendix

I
to the above inequality and obtain:

\displaystyle\|z_{t+1}-z_{t+1}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{z})(1+\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}C_{\mathcal{T}})^{2}\left(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2}\right)\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

(207)

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{c_{z}}\right)(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}L_{RQE}^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}.

Taking
c_{z}=c_{6}\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\|z_{t+1}-z_{t+1}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{6}\alpha_{t}L_{RQE})(1+\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}C_{\mathcal{T}})^{2}\left(1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2}\right)\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}

(208)

\displaystyle+\left(\alpha_{t}^{2}L_{RQE}^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}L_{RQE}}{c_{6}}\right)(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}.

H.3
Solving the Coupled Lyapunov Inequalities

Having obtained the Lyapunov drift inequality (
204
) for
Q
and (
208
) for
z
, we now proceed to solve the coupled Lyapunov drift inequalities. We first simplify (
204
) as follows: For
Q
functions, assume the iterates
\mathbf{Q}_{t}
are bounded, the coefficient with term
\alpha_{t}^{2}
is bounded, and we can write (
204
) as:

\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t+1}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]\leq(1-C_{1}\alpha_{t})\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|^{2}_{\max}+C_{2}\alpha_{t}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+C_{3}\alpha_{t}^{2}

(209)

where

\displaystyle C_{1}=

\displaystyle(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}-\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2};

(210)

\displaystyle C_{2}=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{2d_{t+1,\max{}}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right);

(211)

\displaystyle C_{3}=

\displaystyle\max_{t}(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

(212)

\displaystyle+\left((1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})d_{t+1,\max{}}^{2}+\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{8}{K}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{Q})^{2}.

Similarly, we can rewrite (
208
) as:

\|z_{t+1}-z_{t+1}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+C_{4}\alpha_{t})(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+C_{5}\alpha_{t}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}\|_{\max}^{2}+C_{6}\alpha_{t}^{2}

(213)

where (for simplicity we take
c_{6}=1
):

\displaystyle C_{4}=

\displaystyle(2C_{\mathcal{T}}+1)L_{RQE};

(214)

\displaystyle C_{5}=

\displaystyle L_{RQE}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2};

(215)

\displaystyle C_{6}=

\displaystyle\max_{t}(2+C_{\mathcal{T}})L_{RQE}^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}+L_{RQE}^{3}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\alpha_{t}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}

(216)

\displaystyle+L_{RQE}^{2}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}.

Let
U_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}]
and
V_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]
, by taking expectations on both sides of (
209
) and (
213
), we obtain:

\displaystyle U_{t+1}+kV_{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-C_{1}\alpha_{t})U_{t}+C_{2}\alpha_{t}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}V_{t}+C_{3}\alpha_{t}^{2}

(217)

\displaystyle+k(1+C_{4}\alpha_{t})(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}V_{t}+kC_{5}\alpha_{t}U_{t}+kC_{6}\alpha_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-(C_{1}-kC_{5})\alpha_{t})U_{t}+(k+(C_{2}+kC_{4})\alpha_{t})(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}V_{t}+(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\{(1-(C_{1}-kC_{5})\alpha_{t}),(1+(C_{4}+C_{2}/k)\alpha_{t})(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}\}(U_{t}+kV_{t})+(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha_{t}^{2},

We can now set conditions of
K
and
k
to simplify the above expression. When
K
satisfies:

K\geq\frac{4\sqrt{2}C}{(1-\gamma_{0})(1-\rho)d_{t+1,\min{}}},

(218)

we have:

\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}{4},

(219)

and when

k=\frac{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}{4L_{RQE}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}}.

(220)

we further have:

\displaystyle C_{1}-kC_{5}\geq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}-\frac{1}{4}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}-\frac{1}{4}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}

(221)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}

so that

\displaystyle 1-(C_{1}-kC_{5})\alpha_{t}\leq 1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t}.

(222)

Also, we have:

\displaystyle C_{4}+\frac{C_{2}}{k}\leq

\displaystyle(2C_{\mathcal{T}}+1)L_{RQE}+\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{2d_{t+1,\max{}}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)\frac{4L_{RQE}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}

(223)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2C_{\mathcal{T}}+1)L_{RQE}+\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{2d_{t+1,\max{}}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}^{2}}+\frac{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}{4}\right)\frac{4L_{RQE}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(2C_{\mathcal{T}}+1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}\right)L_{RQE}+8L_{RQE}\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\frac{d_{t+1,\max{}}}{d_{t+1,\min{}}^{3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}+2C_{\mathcal{T}}+8\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\frac{d_{t+1,\max{}}}{d_{t+1,\min{}}^{3}}\right)L_{RQE}

recall that when
\beta_{t}\leq\min\{\frac{1}{\mu},\frac{\mu}{L_{F}^{2}}\}
, we have
(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}=1-2\beta_{t}\mu+\beta_{t}^{2}L_{F}^{2}\leq 1-\beta_{t}\mu
, and when

(C_{1}-kC_{5}+C_{4}+\frac{C_{2}}{k})\alpha_{t}\leq\mu\beta_{t}+(C_{4}+\frac{C_{2}}{k})\alpha_{t}\beta_{t}

(224)

we would have
1-(C_{1}-kC_{5})\alpha_{t}\geq(1+(C_{4}+C_{2}/k)\alpha_{t})(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}
. Let
\alpha_{t}<1
and combining the conditions above, and then (
224
) simplifies to:

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}-kC_{5}+C_{4}+C_{2}/k}{\mu+C_{4}+C_{2}/k}

(225)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}/2+\left(1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}+2C_{\mathcal{T}}+8\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\frac{d_{t+1,\max{}}}{d_{t+1,\min{}}^{3}}\right)L_{RQE}}{\mu+\left(1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}+2C_{\mathcal{T}}+8\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\frac{d_{t+1,\max{}}}{d_{t+1,\min{}}^{3}}\right)L_{RQE}}.

When (
218
), (
220
) and (
225
) are satisfied, our recursion becomes:

U_{t+1}+kV_{t+1}\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{t+1,\min{}}\alpha_{t})(U_{T}+kV_{t})+(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha_{t}^{2}

(226)

To clean up the recursion, notice that we naturally have
d_{t+1,\max{}}\leq 1
, and by
Section

4.3
and
Section

4.3
, we have

d_{t+1,\min{}}\geq d_{\min{}}:=\underline{\mu}\underline{\pi}

(227)

and by a similar argument to
Section

G.2
, we have
\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{\max}\leq Q_{\max},\textup{sp}(\mathbf{Q}_{t})\leq Q_{span}
and
\|\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}\leq Q_{\max}
, and therefore,
C_{3}
can be rewritten as:

\displaystyle C_{3}=

\displaystyle 4(1-\gamma_{0})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)Q_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha_{t}+\frac{1}{K}+\frac{64C\rho}{(1-\rho)K}+2\left(\frac{C}{(1-\rho)K}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}(1-\tilde{\beta}_{t})^{2}\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{8}{K}Q_{span}^{2},

and
C_{6}
can be written as:

\displaystyle C_{6}=

\displaystyle(2+C_{\mathcal{T}})L_{RQE}^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}(1-\beta)^{2}+L_{RQE}^{3}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\alpha_{t}(1-\beta)^{2}+4L_{RQE}^{2}(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}Q_{\max{}}.

(228)

Additionally,
Equation

225
further simplifies to:

\frac{\beta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\geq\frac{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}/2+\left(1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}+2C_{\mathcal{T}}+\frac{8\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}}{d_{\min{}}^{3}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\right)L_{RQE}}{\mu+\left(1+(1+\gamma_{0})^{2}+2C_{\mathcal{T}}+\frac{8\gamma^{2}C_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}}{d_{\min{}}^{3}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{0}}{1-\gamma_{0}}\right)^{2}\right)L_{RQE}}.

(229)

Now by applying
Appendix

I
, we obtain:

Scenario 1:
Assuming we are using constant step sizes
\alpha_{t}=\alpha
,
\beta_{t}=\beta
, we have:

U_{t}+kV_{t}\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha)^{t}(U_{0}+kV_{0})+\frac{2(C_{3}+kC_{6})}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}}\alpha

(230)

and therefore,

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}]\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha)^{t}(Q_{\max}^{2}+8k)+\frac{2(C_{3}+kC_{6})}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}}\alpha

(231)

and

\mathbb{E}[\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq(1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha)^{t}(Q_{\max}^{2}/k+8)+\frac{2(C_{3}/k+C_{6})}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}}\alpha

(232)

and since

\displaystyle\|z_{t}-z^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}+\|z_{t}^{*}-z^{*}\|_{2})^{2}

(233)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}+L_{RQE}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+2L_{RQE}^{2}\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}

we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|z_{t}-z^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}[\|z_{t}-z_{t}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]+2L_{RQE}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}]

(234)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2+2kL_{RQE}^{2})\left((1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha)^{t}(Q_{\max}^{2}/k+8)+\frac{2(C_{3}/k+C_{6})}{(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}}\alpha\right)

Scenario 2:
Assuming we are using diminishing step sizes
\alpha_{t}=\frac{\alpha}{t+h}
,
\beta_{t}=\frac{\beta}{t+h}
, we have:

U_{t}+kV_{t}\leq\left(U_{0}+kV_{0}+\frac{(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha^{2}}{h^{2}}\frac{h+2-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}{1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{h+1}{h+t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha},

(235)

as a result,

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}^{*}\|_{\max}^{2}]\leq\left(Q_{\max}^{2}+8k+\frac{(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha^{2}}{h^{2}}\frac{h+2-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}{1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{h+1}{h+t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha},

(236)

and

\mathbb{E}[\|z_{t}-z^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq(2/k+2L_{RQE}^{2})\left(Q_{\max}^{2}+8k+\frac{(C_{3}+kC_{6})\alpha^{2}}{h^{2}}\frac{h+2-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}{1-\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{h+1}{h+t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}(1-\gamma_{0})d_{\min{}}\alpha}

(237)

Appendix I
Auxiliary Lemmas

In this section we state and prove some auxiliary lemmas that are used in our proofs.

Lemma I.1
.

Let
\Lambda=\textup{Diag}(\lambda)
, a differentiable mapping
F:\mathcal{Z}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{N}
is
\lambda
-strictly monotone if for all
z\in\mathcal{Z}
,

\Lambda\nabla F(z)+\nabla F(z)^{T}\Lambda\succ 0.

(238)

Further, it is
(\mu,\lambda)
-strongly monotone if and only if for all
z\in\mathcal{Z}
,

\Lambda\nabla F(z)+\nabla F(z)^{T}\Lambda\succeq 2\mu I.

(239)

Here
\nabla F(z)
denotes the Jacobian of
F(z)
.

Proof.

We provide a proof for general
\Lambda
.
For
z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}
and
v=z^{\prime}-z
, let

S(z)=\frac{\Lambda\nabla F(z)+\nabla F(z)^{T}\Lambda}{2},

(240)

define a function
\varphi(t):=z+tv,t\in[0,1]
, we have that
\varphi(0)=z
and
\varphi(1)=z^{\prime}
. Consider the function

g(t):=F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v,

(241)

we have that:

\displaystyle\langle F(z^{\prime})-F(z),z^{\prime}-z\rangle_{\Lambda}

(242)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle F(z^{\prime})-F(z),v\rangle_{\Lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(F(z^{\prime})-F(z)\right)^{T}\Lambda v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(1)-g(0).

For
g(1)-g(0)
, we can write is as the following integral:

\displaystyle g(1)-g(0)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}g^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

(243)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\left(F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v\right)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}v^{T}\nabla F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t.

Also, a symmetric expansion of
\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\left(F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v\right)
yields:

g(1)-g(0)=\int_{0}^{1}v^{T}\Lambda\nabla F(\varphi(t))v\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t.

(244)

Therefore, we have that:

g(1)-g(0)=\int_{0}^{1}v^{T}S(\varphi(t))v\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t.

(245)

From Jacobian condition to monotonicity.

If
S(z)\succ 0
, we have that
v^{T}S(\varphi(t))v>0
for all
t
, and therefore,
\langle F(z^{\prime})-F(z),z^{\prime}-z\rangle_{\Lambda}=g(1)-g(0)>0
,
\lambda
-strict monotonicity holds.

If we further have

S(z)\succeq\mu I,

(246)

it holds that

\displaystyle\langle F(z^{\prime})-F(z),z^{\prime}-z\rangle_{\Lambda}

(247)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(1)-g(0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}v^{T}S(\varphi(t))v\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mu\int_{0}^{1}v^{T}v\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu\|v\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu\|z^{\prime}-z\|_{2}^{2},

indicating that
(\mu,\lambda)
-strong monotonicity holds.

From strong monotonicity to Jacobian Condition.

If
F
is
(\mu,\lambda)
-strongly monotone, for all
t_{1}<t_{2},t_{1},t_{2}\in[0,1]
, we have that

(t_{2}-t_{1})\left(g(t_{2})-g(t_{1})\right)=\langle F(z+t_{2}v)-F(z+t_{1}v),t_{2}v-t_{1}v\rangle_{\Lambda}\geq\mu(t_{2}-t_{1})^{2}\|v\|_{2}^{2}

(248)

and therefore,

g(t_{2})-g(t_{1})\geq\mu(t_{2}-t_{1})\|v\|_{2}^{2}.

(249)

Since
F
is differentiable, we have that
g^{\prime}(t)\geq\|v\|_{2}^{2}
. Now we expand
g^{\prime}(t)
to obtain:

g^{\prime}(t)=\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\left(F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v\right)=v^{T}\nabla F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda v

(250)

and similarly using symmetry,

g^{\prime}(t)=v^{T}\Lambda\nabla F(\varphi(t))v

(251)

we have that:

v^{T}\left(\Lambda\nabla F(\varphi(t))+\nabla F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda\right)v\geq 2\mu v^{T}v,

(252)

that gives:

\Lambda\nabla F(\varphi(t))+\nabla F(\varphi(t))^{T}\Lambda\succeq 2\mu I.

(253)

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma I.2
.

Consider a normal form game with bounded payoff matrices
\mathbf{R}
, under the following cases the RQE of the game is bounded away from zero:

1.

D_{i}(\cdot,\cdot)
are KL-divergence and
\nu_{i}(\cdot)
are log-barrier functions, then
\pi_{i}^{*}\geq\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\epsilon_{i}|\mathcal{A}_{i}|+\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})}
;

2.

D_{i}(\cdot,\cdot)
are reverse KL-divergence and
\nu_{i}(\cdot)
are negative entropy, then
\pi_{i}^{*}\geq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\exp\left(-\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\right)
and
p_{i}\geq\frac{\exp\left(-\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\right)}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|(|\mathcal{A}_{-i}|+\tau_{i}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R}))}
.

Proof.

Consider the gradient operator of the game:

F(z;\mathbf{R})=\begin{bmatrix}-R_{1}p_{1}+\epsilon_{1}\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})\\
-R_{2}p_{2}+\epsilon_{2}\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})\\
R_{1}^{T}\pi_{1}+\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2})\\
R_{2}^{T}\pi_{2}+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1})\end{bmatrix}

(254)

Case 1:
When
D_{i}
are KL-divergence and
\nu_{i}
are log-barrier functions, their gradients can be written as:

\nabla\nu_{i}(\pi_{i})=-(\pi_{i})^{-1};\nabla_{p_{i}}D_{i}(p_{i},\pi_{-i})=\log\frac{p_{i}}{\pi_{-i}}+1.

(255)

Since
\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})
and
\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})
goes to infinity on the boundary of the simplex, the RQE
\pi^{*}
must satisfy the KKT condition for its optimality:

R_{i}p_{i}^{*}-\mu 1=\epsilon_{i}\nabla\nu_{i}(\pi_{i}^{*})=-\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\pi_{i}^{*}}.

(256)

which implies that
\pi_{i}^{*}
has the form:

\pi_{i}^{*}(a)=\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\mu-R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)}

(257)

here we overload the notation
\mu
to denote Lagrange multiplier such that:

\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\mu-R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)}=1.

(258)

Let
a_{\max}=\argmax_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)
, since the probability of choosing
a_{\max}
is at least
\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}
, we have:

\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\mu-R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a_{\max})}\geq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}

(259)

which implies:

\mu\leq R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a_{\max})+\epsilon_{i}|\mathcal{A}_{i}|

(260)

This leads to the conclusion that:

\pi_{i}^{*}(a)\geq\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\epsilon_{i}|\mathcal{A}_{i}|+R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a_{\max})-R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)}\geq\frac{\epsilon_{i}}{\epsilon_{i}|\mathcal{A}_{i}|+\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})}.

(261)

Case 2:
When
D_{i}
are reverse KL-divergence and
\nu_{i}
are negative entropy, their gradients can be written as:

\nabla\nu_{i}(\pi_{i})=\log\pi_{i}+1;\nabla_{p_{i}}D_{i}(p_{i},\pi_{-i})=-\frac{\pi_{-i}}{p_{i}}.

(262)

Similarly, we have that

\pi_{i}^{*}(a)=\frac{\exp\left(\frac{R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)}{\epsilon_{i}}\right)}{\sum_{a^{\prime}}\exp\left(\frac{R_{i}p_{i}^{*}(a)}{\epsilon_{i}}\right)}\geq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\exp\left(-\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\right)

(263)

and additionally we have:

p_{i}\geq\frac{\exp\left(-\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\right)}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|(|\mathcal{A}_{-i}|+\tau_{i}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R}))}.

(264)

∎

Lemma I.3
.

Let
F
be a strictly
\lambda
-monotone mapping and
z^{*}
be the unique solution to the
\lambda
-weighted variational inequality:

\langle z^{*}-z,F(z^{*})\rangle_{\lambda}\leq 0,\forall z\in\Delta

(265)

in some closed convex set
\Delta
, let
Proj
denote the Euclidean projection onto
\Delta
, we have that:

\textup{Proj}(z^{*}-\eta\Lambda F(z^{*}))=z^{*},

(266)

where
\Lambda=\textup{Diag}(\lambda)
.

Proof.

Recall that Euclidean projection
\hat{z}
of
z^{*}-\eta\Lambda F(z^{*})
is characterized by:

\langle z^{*}-\eta\Lambda F(z^{*})-\hat{z},z-\hat{z}\rangle\leq 0,\forall z\in\Delta

(267)

Notice that the optimality condition of
z_{t}^{*}
:

\langle z^{*}-z,F(z^{*})\rangle_{\lambda}=(z^{*}-z)^{T}\Lambda F(z^{*})\leq 0,\forall z\in\Delta

(268)

would immediately imply that taking
\hat{z}=z^{*}
satisfies the characterization, conclude that
z^{*}
is the projection.
∎

Lemma I.4
.

If the payoff matrix
\mathbf{R}
is bounded and the regularizers
\nu_{i}(\cdot)
are
S_{\nu}
-smooth and
D_{i}(p_{i},\pi_{-i})
are
S_{D}
-smooth, then the gradient operator given by:

F(z;\mathbf{R})=\begin{bmatrix}-R_{1}p_{1}+\epsilon_{1}\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})\\
-R_{2}p_{2}+\epsilon_{2}\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})\\
R_{1}^{T}\pi_{1}+\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2})\\
R_{2}^{T}\pi_{2}+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1})\end{bmatrix}

(269)

is
L_{F}
-Lipschitz continuous for

L_{F}=2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{1}||\mathcal{A}_{2}|}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})+(\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2})S_{\nu}+(\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}})S_{D}.

(270)

Proof.

To prove the Lipschitz continuity of
F
, we consider the difference between
F(z;\mathbf{R})
and
F(z^{\prime};\mathbf{R})
for
z=(\pi_{1},\pi_{2},p_{1},p_{2})
and
z^{\prime}=(\pi_{1}^{\prime},\pi_{2}^{\prime},p_{1}^{\prime},p_{2}^{\prime})
. We have:

\displaystyle\|F(z;\mathbf{R})-F(z^{\prime};\mathbf{R})\|_{2}

(271)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}-R_{1}(p_{1}-p_{1}^{\prime})+\epsilon_{1}(\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})-\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1}^{\prime}))\\
-R_{2}(p_{2}-p_{2}^{\prime})+\epsilon_{2}(\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})-\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2}^{\prime}))\\
R_{1}^{T}(\pi_{1}-\pi_{1}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}(\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2})-\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1}^{\prime},\pi_{2}^{\prime}))\\
R_{2}^{T}(\pi_{2}-\pi_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}(\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1})-\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2}^{\prime},\pi_{1}^{\prime}))\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}-R_{1}(p_{1}-p_{1}^{\prime})\\
-R_{2}(p_{2}-p_{2}^{\prime})\\
R_{1}^{T}(\pi_{1}-\pi_{1}^{\prime})\\
R_{2}^{T}(\pi_{2}-\pi_{2}^{\prime})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\epsilon_{1}(\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})-\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1}^{\prime}))\\
\epsilon_{2}(\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})-\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2}^{\prime}))\\
\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}(\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2})-\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1}^{\prime},\pi_{2}^{\prime}))\\
\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}(\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1})-\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2}^{\prime},\pi_{1}^{\prime}))\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{1}||\mathcal{A}_{2}|}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\|z-z^{\prime}\|_{2}+\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1})-\nabla\nu_{1}(\pi_{1}^{\prime})\|_{2}+\epsilon_{2}\|\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2})-\nabla\nu_{2}(\pi_{2}^{\prime})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}\|\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1},\pi_{2})-\nabla_{p_{1}}D_{1}(p_{1}^{\prime},\pi_{2}^{\prime})\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}}\|\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2},\pi_{1})-\nabla_{p_{2}}D_{2}(p_{2}^{\prime},\pi_{1}^{\prime})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{1}||\mathcal{A}_{2}|}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})\|z-z^{\prime}\|_{2}+\epsilon_{1}S_{\nu}\|\pi_{1}-\pi_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}+\epsilon_{2}S_{\nu}\|\pi_{2}-\pi_{2}^{\prime}\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{S_{D}}{\tau_{1}}\|(p_{1},\pi_{2})-(p_{1}^{\prime},\pi_{2}^{\prime})\|_{2}+\frac{S_{D}}{\tau_{2}}\|(p_{2},\pi_{1})-(p_{2}^{\prime},\pi_{1}^{\prime})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{1}||\mathcal{A}_{2}|}\textup{sp}(\mathbf{R})+(\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2})S_{\nu}+(\frac{1}{\tau_{1}}+\frac{1}{\tau_{2}})S_{D}\right)\|z-z^{\prime}\|_{2},

where we have used the fact that
\pi_{i},p_{i}
are on the simplex in (i). This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma I.5
.

[Covariance bound for
\alpha
-mixing sequences, Lemma 1.2 in
(
Ibragimov 1962
)
]
Let
U
and
V
be bounded random variables such that
U
is measurable with respect to

\mathcal{F}_{-\infty}^{t}
and
V
with respect to
\mathcal{F}_{t+k}^{+\infty}
.
Then

\big|\operatorname{Cov}(U,V)\big|\;\leq\;4\,\alpha(k)\,\|U\|_{\infty}\,\|V\|_{\infty},

(272)

where

\alpha(k)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}\sup_{A\in\mathcal{F}_{-\infty}^{t},B\in\mathcal{F}_{t+k}^{+\infty}}|\Pr(A\cap B)-\Pr(A)\Pr(B)|

(273)

\alpha(k)
is the
\alpha
-mixing coefficient at lag
k
.

Lemma I.6
.

[
(
Meyn & Tweedie 2012
)
]
If a Markov chain
\{X_{t}\}
is uniformly geometrically ergodic, then the chain is geometrically
\alpha
-mixing with
\alpha(k)\leq 2C\rho^{k}
.

Lemma I.7
.

For real numbers
a,b
and constant
c>0
, it holds that:

(a+b)^{2}\leq(1+c)a^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{c})b^{2}.

(274)

Furthermore, for real matrices
A
and
B
, it holds that:

\|A+B\|_{\max}^{2}\leq(1+c)\|A\|_{\max}^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{c})\|B\|_{\max}^{2}.

(275)

Proof.

We have that

\displaystyle(a+b)^{2}=

\displaystyle a^{2}+2ab+b^{2}

(276)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a^{2}+b^{2}+2(\sqrt{c}a\cdot\frac{b}{\sqrt{c}})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle(1+c)a^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{c})b^{2}

where (i) comes from the AM-GM inequality. The inequality for the matrix holds by applying the above inequality element-wise:

\displaystyle(A_{ij}+B_{ij})^{2}\leq(1+c)A_{ij}^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{c})B_{ij}^{2}\leq(1+c)\|A\|_{\max}^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{c})\|B\|_{\max}^{2}.

(277)

and the result holds because the inequality above holds for all
i,j
.
∎

Lemma I.8
.

Consider a series
\{w_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying the following inequality:

w_{t+1}\leq(1-k\alpha_{t})w_{t}+C\alpha_{t}^{2}

(278)

for some
k>0
such that
k\alpha_{t}<1
, we have for all
t
:

w_{t}\leq(1-k\alpha)^{t}w_{0}+\frac{C}{k}\alpha,

(279)

if we use constant step size
\alpha_{t}=\alpha
, and

w_{t}\leq\left(w_{0}+\frac{C\alpha^{2}}{h^{2}}\frac{h+2-k\alpha}{1-k\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{h+1}{h+t}\right)^{k\alpha},

(280)

if we use diminishing step sizes
\alpha_{t}=\frac{\alpha}{t+h}
.

Proof.

Unrolling the recursion we obtain:

\displaystyle w_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha_{T})w_{T}+C\alpha_{T}^{2}

(281)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha_{T})(1-k\alpha_{T-1})w_{T-1}+C\alpha_{T}^{2}+C(1-k\alpha_{T})\alpha_{T-1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dots

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{T}(1-k\alpha_{t})w_{0}+C\sum_{t=0}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{2}\prod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1-k\alpha_{s}).

For constant step size
\alpha_{t}=\alpha
, the inequality above can be simplified as:

\displaystyle w_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha)^{T+1}w_{0}+C\sum_{t=0}^{T}\alpha^{2}(1-k\alpha)^{T-t}

(282)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha)^{T+1}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T}(1-k\alpha)^{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha)^{T+1}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\frac{1-(1-k\alpha)^{T+1}}{k\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-k\alpha)^{T+1}w_{0}+\frac{C}{k}\alpha.

For deminishing step sizes
\alpha_{t}=\frac{\alpha}{t+h}
, we have:

\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{T}(1-k\alpha_{t})=

\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{T}(1-\frac{k\alpha}{t+h})

(283)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\log(1-\frac{k\alpha}{t+h})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k\alpha\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{1}{t+h}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k\alpha(H_{h+T}-H_{h-1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}

and similarly,

\displaystyle\prod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1-k\alpha_{s})=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\log(1-\frac{k\alpha}{s+h})\right)

(284)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k\alpha\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\frac{1}{s+h}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k\alpha(H_{h+T}-H_{h+t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h+t+1}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}.

Therefore, the bound for
w_{T+1}
can be simplified as:

\displaystyle w_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{1}{(t+h)^{2}}\left(\frac{h+t+1}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}

(285)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{(h+T+1)^{k\alpha}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{(t+h+1)^{k\alpha}}{(t+h)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{(h+T+1)^{k\alpha}}\left(\frac{h+1}{h}\right)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T}(t+h+1)^{k\alpha-2}

Notice that:

\sum_{t=0}^{T}(t+h+1)^{k\alpha-2}\leq(h+1)^{k\alpha-2}+\int_{h+1}^{h+T+2}x^{k\alpha-2}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\leq(h+1)^{k\alpha-2}+\frac{(h+1)^{k\alpha-1}}{1-k\alpha}

(286)

we obtain the final bound of:

\displaystyle w_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{h}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}w_{0}+C\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{(h+T+1)^{k\alpha}}\left(\frac{h+1}{h}\right)^{2}\left((h+1)^{k\alpha-2}+\frac{(h+1)^{k\alpha-1}}{1-k\alpha}\right)

(287)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(w_{0}+\frac{C\alpha^{2}}{h^{2}}\frac{h+2-k\alpha}{1-k\alpha}\right)\left(\frac{h+1}{h+T+1}\right)^{k\alpha}

∎
